# Project Phoenix — the implementation

> **This is the one file that changes every week. The other three do not.** The specification says
> what is TRUE of the world; `ARCHITECTURE.md` says what the code IS; `COVERAGE.md` says what of the
> specification the code MEETS. None of them names an item in this file, ever: a coverage row says
> what is short, never who fixes it; a source comment says why, never which item; a noun's home
> names the mechanism that houses it, never the item that builds one. A reference from a fixed file
> into a moving one is dead the week after it is written, and `npm run check:existence` refuses it.
>
> **Parts 1–2 are the order; Part 4 is the exhaustive clause ledger.** Take the first open item in
> Part 2 whose prerequisites are met. Finish it — checks green, `world:runs` green, COVERAGE re-marked,
> Part 4 regenerated — before opening the next. A new idea is INSERTED at its dependency position,
> never appended, and the insertion says where and why.
>
> **NOTHING DONE IS IN THIS FILE.** When an item closes, delete it. What it produced is the code.
>
> **THIS IS WHERE A FINDING LIVES.** Part 0.3 is the ledger of what the last verification pass
> measured. Each finding sits under the item that fixes it; a finding that fits no item becomes one,
> inserted where its dependencies put it. A finding leaves this file only by being fixed.
>
> **Every block below opens with what to read first.** The specification section, the laws it
> leans on, and the production source. Do not implement from the item text alone: the item says
> what closes it, the specification says what it must be.

## Part 0 — The measured state

### 0.2 What the world actually does

`npm run world:runs` is the reachability gate: it steps the arbitrary scale world four weeks and
asserts only that nothing throws. It is **green** — 10,318 parties, 16,750 instruments, 1,546 books,
51 systems in 60 phases, four weeks, worst week 7.0 s against the 3.0 s the migration was judged on.

That world is ARBITRARY and declared arbitrary: every number is a counter, nothing was cleared or
decided, and it is not a seed. It proves that every module is reached, that the phase order holds
at scale, and that what a mechanism proposes goes over the wire. It proves nothing about behaviour,
and findings F1–F4 are what it reports every week. Item 13 replaces it.

## Part 1 — The order

| #     | milestone                                                                  | dependency reason                                                                                         |
| ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **0** | **Make the record true and the gate green**                                | every later item re-marks COVERAGE and commits on `world:runs`; neither can be trusted today              |
| 1     | Make kernel contracts, market state and the week's order unambiguous       | every place, route, derivative, cure, delivery, benchmark and audit reads these identities and this order |
| 2     | Finish sovereign funding and the transacted benchmark                      | Part XIII places a sovereign that can fail before anything priced over its curve                          |
| 3     | Finish firm, household, labour and goods operating flows                   | credit, ratings and loss need real cash flow and named counterparties                                     |
| 4     | Finish credit rows, loss rights and resolution                             | loans, collateral, recovery and failure must exist before risk transfer                                   |
| 5     | Finish redeemable claims, equity, dealers and cost-of-capital transmission | forced selling and investment require real claims, inventory limits and cleared prices                    |
| 6     | Finish bank funding, capital and resolution as one balance sheet           | deposits, reserves, collateral and capital must reconcile                                                 |
| 7     | Finish currency, cross-border and benchmark curves                         | derivatives and foreign funding require named currencies, pairs and transacted references                 |
| 8     | Finish reporting, estimates, housing, employment and corporate control     | these consume stable accounting, price and credit layers and supply the polity                            |
| 9     | Finish polity and policy transmission                                      | votes require cell-level lived outcomes; mandates change only policy-owned primitives                     |
| 10    | Finish derivatives and risk transfer, one typed contract at a time         | CDS, IRS, FX derivatives, futures and securitisation require all earlier underlyings and failure states   |
| 11    | Complete the cell life cycle and physical delivery                         | entry, death, promotion and merge, and goods in transit, must preserve ownership and relationships        |
| 12    | Close and audit the sixteen causal chains                                  | only now do all endpoints exist                                                                           |
| 13    | Generate the calibrated opening world                                      | seed primitives, histories and reasons only after the consuming mechanisms are stable                     |
| 14    | Measure, optimise, then build the observer and the app                     | Laws 11 and 18 put diagnosis and performance after behaviour                                              |

## Part 2 — The items

The week every item is placed against is Money G2, whose nine stages carry internal orders of their
own. Written out, that is **31 slots**, and every system sits in exactly one:

```
a1 offers expire → a2 payments given up as fails
b1 what accrues accrues → b2 due paid or arrear → b3 loss lands on named holders → b4 party ceases → b5 estate distributes
c1 entry → c2 death → c3 promotion → c4 split → c5 merge
d1 lines draw inputs → d2 batches finish → d3 plant wears → d4 goods move → d5 engagements made and ended → d6 whoever is short brings paper
e1 every outlook is formed, once → e2 every participant posts
f  the books clear, once, per market and instrument
g1 positions marked → g2 gains and losses landed → g3 derived levels read → g4 constraints tested → g5 accounts published → g6 opinions formed → g7 made visible
h  obligations for the week after, and nothing else
i1 gridlock pass → i2 audit
```

A fact produced at one slot is readable this week only by a later slot. Every feedback loop in the
model therefore has at least one edge that crosses the week boundary, and that edge is its period.
Two systems meet _simultaneously_ in one place only — as counterparties in a book — and a book is
`(market, subject instrument, currency, venue)`. No system calls another; A writes a store, B reads
it. The twelve channels are `MechanismContext`'s write doors and there is no thirteenth.

---

### 0. Make the record true and the gate green

**Read before this block:** Money G1–G2, Clearing A2–A4 and C4, Audit A1 and B5, Appendix C, Laws
4, 6, 10, 12, 16, 19. Then `clearing.rs`, `protocols.rs`, `treasury.rs` (`TreasuryIssues`),
`module.rs` (`Participant`, `ParticipantView`), `tools/coverage-existence.ts`, `tools/plan-gaps.ts`.

- [ ] **0.2 `posted()` reports what was posted.** `NoOverlap.best_bid` becomes `Option<f64>`, or the
      outcome becomes `NoDemand` when no bid carries a level, exactly as `book()` already does. Fixes F5.
- [ ] **0.3 No fixed file names an item.** Strip every `(item …)` and bare item id from
      `docs/COVERAGE.md` (119 references), `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` (item 24), `audit.rs` (`waits_on`),
      `assembly.rs` (noun `why` strings, noun `home` values) and any source comment. A PARTIAL row says
      what is short; a noun's home names the store or mechanism that houses it. Invert the tool:
      `check:existence` refuses `\bitem \d` anywhere in COVERAGE, ARCHITECTURE and `packages/kernel-rs/src`,
      and drops the "PARTIAL must name an item" rule. Delete `COVERAGE.md` header's `coverage:spec`
      sentence and the phantom rows `Money D5`, `XI-11`. Amend the ontology sentence in `CLAUDE.md` to
      match. Fixes F11, F12 (the strings), F32.
- [ ] **0.4 A MET row cites a reached item.** Add `npm run check:reach`: every `pub` item in
      `packages/kernel-rs/src` referenced nowhere in production (own module counted, `#[cfg(test)]`
      and `src/bin` excluded) is listed, and a MET row whose cited path is one of them is refused. Then
      re-mark **row by row, no shared string**: Sovereign (52), Geography (42), Treasury (25), Money
      (37), Register (26), Clearing (27), Expectations (21), Commodities Spot (18). Where the mechanism
      is unreached the row is MISSING and names the unreached item. Recount 0.1 from the tool, never by
      hand. Fixes F17, F19, F34.
- [ ] **0.5 Every clause has a row and every row counts.** Give the 396 NOTE-form sub-clauses a row
      (`npm run coverage:spec` prints the missing ids; add that script). `coverage-existence.ts` and
      `plan-gaps.ts` stop filtering `form !== 'NOTE'`, so the 37 orphans enter Part 4 and the 0.1
      table. A NOTE inherits its parent's mark only when its own text is met; otherwise it is marked on
      its own. Recount. Fixes F6, F7.
- [ ] **0.6 Re-mark the tautologies.** `Audit B5` → MISSING (_"no equity account exists"_); `Audit
B5.a` gets its row. Check every MET whose contribution compares a value with `None` or with itself
      and re-mark it. Fixes F8's record; 1.4 fixes the mechanism.
- [ ] **0.7 The nouns say where a fact lives.** Re-declare the three homeless nouns with `home:
Some("<the store that will house it>")` and the twenty-three settled ones with no item id in
      `why`. `Nouns` keeps refusing an empty home. 1.8 makes the register a check.
- [ ] **0.8 Delete every stale statement found.** `audit.rs:1299-1313` (the diary and the line
      `world:runs` no longer prints), `Family::waits_on` (all ten are built — delete the method and
      `NotBuilt`'s dependence on it, keep `NotBuilt`), `prices.rs:130` (_"carries its own week"_),
      `prices.rs:56` and `ARCHITECTURE.md:200` (the print key, until 1.2 makes them true),
      `ARCHITECTURE.md:249` (Law 48 → Appendix B #48), `:284` (`Dispatches` is on `Settlement`),
      `money_market.rs` struct doc (the price is not the bank's own until 2.5). A comment says why; a
      comment that says what was is deleted. Fixes F16, F32.
- [ ] **0.9 `phoenix-check` matches the code's idiom.** Add to `KINDS`: `kind_of(` compared with
      `kinds::`; to `BOUNDS`: the `if a > b { a } else { b }` and `if x > 0.0 { x } else { 0.0 }`
      shapes outside the waterfall arithmetic that names its reason; to `DEFAULTS`: `unwrap_or(&0`,
      `unwrap_or(&0.0`, and `unwrap_or(<call>)` on an `Outcome` field. Each new pattern lands with its
      current count as a **ratchet** in `RATCHETS`, and the items below lower it. Fixes the checker half
      of F25, F30.
- [ ] **0.10 The existence table survives formatting.** `coverage-existence.ts` finds the table by
      `indexOf` of an unpadded header string, so `npm run format` pads it and the gate stops finding it.
      Parse the table by its header cells instead, and have `check` run `format:check` so the two
      cannot disagree again. Fixes F35.

### 1. Make kernel contracts, market state and the week's order unambiguous

**Read before this block:** Money G2 in full, Clearing D–F, Register D3–D5, Audit B5 and B8, Part
XII's Units family, XI-5, XI-6, XI-14, XI-15, §49 in full, Laws 2, 4, 5, 8, 10, 15, 19. Then
`world.rs`, `assembly.rs` (`World::step`, `wire_up`, `split_cell`, `phase`), `module.rs`,
`session.rs`, `prices.rs`, `register.rs`, `ledger.rs`, `parties.rs`, `nouns.rs`, `params.rs`,
`geography.rs`, `registry.rs`, `systems.rs`.

- [ ] **1.1 Every phase declares what it reads and writes.** `systems::works/posts/reads` take
      `reads: &[Produces]`, `writes: &[Produces]`; `phase()` loses its empty defaults. `Phases::seal`
      checks **every** writer of a `Produces`, not the first, and refuses two writers of one (Law 4).
      Closes when at least one wrong placement is refused at assembly. Fixes F20.
- [ ] **1.2 A print is keyed `(market, instrument, week)`.** `Prints::at` keys on both; `latest`
      takes the market. A carried print keeps `struck: u32` — the week it last cleared — beside `week`,
      so age is a read. `prices.rs`, `ARCHITECTURE.md` §6 then say what is true. Fixes F28; Commodities
      Spot A1/D1 re-marked.
- [ ] **1.3 The holder declares how it carries a position.** `MechanismContext::carries(line,
Carrying)` and the same on `ParticipantView`; the wire never assigns a treatment — a credit into
      an undeclared row throws with XI-6's citation. `opening.rs` and `assembly.rs` keep their doors.
      Delete `ledger.rs:1348`; the read that replaces it is `register.carrying(row)`. Fixes F27.
- [ ] **1.4 The equity account.** A per-party `Equity` balance in `stores.rs`, moved only by
      `Leg::Money` receipts of kind capital-in, by retained income at g5 and by booked losses at b3/g2
      — never set. `audit.rs` replaces `BookedAccountsReadable` with a contribution comparing
      `instruments::booked_equity` (the residual) against the account, with derived dust. Audit B5, B5.a,
      B5.b re-marked. Fixes F8.
- [ ] **1.5 The Units family owns the population.** Move the three cell contributions to
      `Family::Units`. Add `CellHoldingsDivideByWeight`: for every live cell, every holding total and
      every lien quantity is `weight × integer per-member`, to the dust of the walk. Fixes F14 (the
      check), F15.
- [ ] **1.6 A split moves the members' share of everything.** `split_cell` moves encumbered units
      and re-creates each lien on the child pro rata; both cells' totals stay divisible. Fixes F14.
- [ ] **1.7 A weight event is recorded.** `reweigh` writes the `PopulationEvent` it is named with;
      `split` and `merge` record one each. `population_history` becomes the audit's read. Fixes F13's
      record half; milestone 11 gives the four missing events their callers.
- [ ] **1.8 The ontology register is a check.** At `wire_up`, every `World` field, every `Standing`
      kind and every `Journal` kind is passed through `Nouns::sort_of`, so a store nobody declared
      throws at assembly. Fixes F12.
- [ ] **1.9 A participant posts where it is declared.** Orders are collected at e2 (`VIEWS`, after
      `expectations`) and cleared at f. `posts(...)` asserts `at == AT_VIEWS`; `money_market` moves to
      `AT_VIEWS` with §11 A3.a satisfied by reading the position the week's flows left. Fixes F22.
- [ ] **1.10 Capacity refuses; it does not abort.** `Leg::Dispatch` over capacity is
      `Outcome::NoCapacity` recorded on the wire, not a panic. Fixes the first half of F31.
- [ ] **1.11 Every shape says it is one.** Delete `Owner::Model`. Each of the 35 numbers it owned
      becomes one of: party state drawn once at entry (a preference, dispersed), a policy with a named
      owner, a technology, or a `Placeholder { mechanism, item: "" }` naming the mechanism whose absence
      it stands in for — `broker.could_move` (§16 D1), `household.will_spend.*` (§41 C1),
      `pool.pools` (§42 D2), `equity.payout` (§10 D3.b), `dealer.limit` (§26 D1), `firm.buffer`,
      `money_market.*` (deleted by 2.5). `world:runs` prints a nonzero count that falls. Fixes F9.
- [ ] **1.12 The week has 31 slots.** `world.rs` gains the sub-orders of G2.a/b/c/d/e/g/i as data
      (`Slot`), `Phases::add` takes a slot, and every row in `systems::all` is placed by the table in
      Part 3. `AT_POPULATION` exists as c1–c5 and is where 11's events run. `propose_due` gains
      `propose`'s `SCHEDULED` refusal. Fixes F21, F33; `Money G2.b/c/e/g` re-marked.
- [ ] **1.13 Geography is in the world.** `World.geography`; the opening state declares countries,
      regions and sites through `geography.rs`'s writers; `registry.rs`'s `CountryId`/region reads go
      **through** geography (delete the duplicate `CountryId`; the read that replaces it is
      `geography.jurisdiction_of`). Nine `impl Contribution` for §49 H1. Re-mark all 42 rows from what is
      reached. Fixes F18.

### 2. Finish sovereign funding and the transacted benchmark

**Read before this block:** Sovereign in full, Treasury D–E, Central Bank B–D, Money Market A–C,
Indices D3–D5, Bond N5–N7, XI-7, XI-9, Laws 3, 4, 19. Then `sovereign.rs`, `treasury.rs`,
`money_market.rs`, `benchmarks.rs`, `bank_funding.rs`, `schedules` in `stores.rs`.

- [ ] **2.1 Accrual is a kernel pass at b1.** One pass over `Schedules` × each line's declared
      convention (Bond N6): coupons, bill accretion against the line's own cleared price (Sovereign F2),
      loan interest, CDS premium, IRS legs, fund fees, mortgage interest. The accrued is a receivable on
      the holder's sheet and a payable on the issuer's, read by b2. `Money G2.b` re-marked MET.
- [ ] **2.2 Sovereign's dead helpers are wired or deleted.** `clear_uniform_auction` is deleted —
      the read is `clearing::clear` with `BuyersCompete`, which is the uniform-price auction.
      `annual_yield_from_price` runs at g3 and its print is the curve's observation. `coupon_payments`
      is 2.1. `central_bank_remittance` runs at b2 as a payment to the treasury. `pledgeable_value` is
      read by the money market's collateral test. `BondFuture` and the basis trade move to 10.6 and
      their rows to MISSING. Fixes F19 (sovereign).
- [ ] **2.3 Primary dealers with an obligation to bid.** A `Participant` for dealers holding the
      privilege: it must post into every sovereign auction, at its own reservation, within its own
      position limit, and the obligation's cost is its own P&L. Sovereign C3, C3.a, C3.b, D6 re-marked.
- [ ] **2.4 Treasury's dead helpers are wired or deleted.** `debt_reconciles` becomes an audit
      contribution (Treasury D6); `interest_reaches` is a read of the wire (F3); `cost_of_issuing` is
      read at g3 (E3); `rollover_exposure`, `central_bank_buys` wired to §31's operation or deleted with
      the read named. Fixes F19 (treasury).
- [ ] **2.5 The money-market price is each bank's own.** Delete `money_market.lends_at` and
      `borrows_at`. A lender posts at its blended cost of funds (Banks Funding B2) plus its own view of
      the borrower's name (§11 B2, `expectations::About::RatePerAnnum` per counterparty); a borrower
      posts at what its alternatives cost (the corridor's ceiling, its collateral). Two books — secured
      and unsecured — and a term book beside overnight. The central bank's facility is a seat at the top
      of the corridor bounded by unencumbered eligible paper (§31 D1). Fixes F10.
- [ ] **2.6 The benchmarks are declared, weighted and two.** `benchmarks.rs:466` reads
      `registry.indices` for the sovereign curve's constituents, never party kind. Producer prices over
      `registry.made()` weighted by production; consumer prices over the goods households actually
      bought, weighted by cell-weighted expenditure, rent weighted by the cell's weight, freight and tax
      included; both with a declared base and chained across a change of constituents (§22 B2.a). The
      weights are `Params` declarations, not literals. Fixes F25 (benchmarks), F29; Goods G1, G1.c and
      Indices A4, B2.a re-marked.

### 3. Finish firm, household, labour and goods operating flows

**Read before this block:** Firm, Goods, Freight, Labour, Housing, Households, Capital Programme,

`goods.rs`, `freight.rs`, `employment.rs`, `housing.rs`, `households.rs`, `capital_programme.rs`,

      `insurers`, `securities_lending` (d5), `treasury`, `short_term_debt`, `corporate_credit`,
      `equity`, `securitisation` (d6). The treasury sizes its need **after** the week's depreciation and
      payroll have landed.
- [ ] **3.2 Goods downstream of the line.** Wire `carry` (lower of cost and net realisable value,
      write-down as a dated income event), `Consignment::landed_cost`, cost of goods sold into the
      income statement. Goods E2, E2.c, E3, F5 re-marked.
- [ ] **3.3 Labour clears on the wage.** `Posting` as a bid at the employer's wage, `matching` as
      `clear` with `BuyersCompete` and pro-rata ties, `Separation`/`Ended` as events, the seeker's
      reservation from the benefit it is paid. Every hire and separation of part of a cell splits it at
      c4. The Labour block in Part 4.
- [ ] **3.4 Housing moves dwellings.** Bids from what §23 will lend, offers from tenure ending and
      completions, a cross, a `Leg::Asset` for the dwelling, rent as a scheduled obligation, foreclosure
      returning supply to the book. The Housing block in Part 4.
- [ ] **3.5 A wage takes one path.** `employment.rs:512`'s branch on the employer's kind becomes a
      `KindProfile` capability (`originates_payroll_by_contract`); the treasury's payroll and a firm's
      are one mechanism reading it. Fixes F25 (employment).
- [ ] **3.6 Transit is a property of the route.** `Leg::Dispatch.arrives` is computed from the
      route's declared legs and the carrier's speed (§49 F4), never `week + 1`. Fixes the second half of
      F31.
- [ ] **3.7 The remaining Part 4 clauses of Firm, Goods, Freight, Labour, Housing, Households,
      Capital Programme, Commodities Spot and Expectations**, in the order Part 4 lists them.

- [ ] **7.1 The FX pair set clears jointly.** One solve over all `n(n−1)/2` pair books with
      cross-consistency as a **constraint** (§12 C2.a); the residual inconsistency is left standing as a
      measurable arbitrage that bounded participants may or may not close (§12 E3). This is the one
      place two books are not independent, and `run_books` gains a joint path for it.

      promoted at c3 (§42 A6.c).

## Part 3 — Where every system sits in the week

The target placement for item 1.12, derived from Money G2.a–i. A row's slot is where its
**mechanism** runs; a participant posts at e2 whatever else its system does.

| slot   | systems                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| a1, a2 | kernel only                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| b1     | the accrual pass — every dated claim by its own convention (2.1)                                                                                                                                              |
| b2     | `lending` (servicing), `cds` premium, `treasury` outlays, remittance                                                                                                                                          |
| b3     | `loss`, `cds` event, `trade_credit` loss, `housing` foreclosure                                                                                                                                               |
| b4     | `mortality` — every kind's own trigger                                                                                                                                                                        |
| b5     | `estate`                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| c1–c5  | entry, death, promotion, split, merge (11.1–11.5)                                                                                                                                                             |
| d1     | `recipe`, `goods` draws, `commodities` production, `freight` voyages                                                                                                                                          |
| d2     | `goods` batches finish                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| d3     | `capital_programme`                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| d4     | `freight` dispatch and arrival                                                                                                                                                                                |
| d5     | `employment`, `housing` tenancies and mortgages, `trade_credit`, `small_business`, `insurers`, `securities_lending`, `prime_brokerage`, `private_equity` calls                                                |
| d6     | `treasury`, `short_term_debt`, `corporate_credit`, `equity`, `securitisation`, `bank_capital` raise, `bank_funding` wholesale                                                                                 |
| e1     | `expectations`, and nothing before it                                                                                                                                                                         |
| e2     | every `Participant` — `households`, `stockists`, `funds`, `insurers`, `dealing`, `hedge_funds`, `treasury`, `money_market`, `capital_programme`, `forced_sale`, `goods` sellers, §8's dealers, §22's trackers |
| f      | kernel: one solver per book; the FX pair set jointly (7.1); a derivative's strike with its cut and margin (10.1)                                                                                              |
| g1     | marks — `money` read, every position through its declared treatment                                                                                                                                           |
| g2     | `firms` result, FX revaluation (7.3), variation margin, inventory write-down                                                                                                                                  |
| g3     | `benchmarks`, `indices`, `consumer_prices` and producer prices, `cost_of_capital`, `spot_fx` cross rates, `cross_border`, the sovereign curve, the swap curve                                                 |
| g4     | `bank_capital`, `bank_funding`, `prime_brokerage`, `hedge_funds`, `derivative_layer`, `redeemable` NAV, mandate tests                                                                                         |
| g5     | `reporting`, `firms`' published expectation                                                                                                                                                                   |
| g6     | `ratings`, `second_opinion`, each bank's estimate                                                                                                                                                             |
| g7     | `observer`, last                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| h      | `private_equity`, `forced_sale` (the call), `control`, `polity`, margin calls, covenant demands, redemptions                                                                                                  |
| i1, i2 | kernel only                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

The loops the placement closes, with their periods in weeks: XI-2 forced seller (1 per hop); §26
dealer inventory → quote (1); §22 index inclusion (1); XI-16 expectation (1, damped by memory); §36
supply-network contagion (1 per hop); §16/§28 margin spiral (1 per turn); §24 bank run (1–2); XI-12
currency ↔ basis (1–2); XI-1 loss → capital → lending (2); §44 downgrade (2); §40 housing (2);
§21/§20 inventory → curve (2); XI-11 risk transfer → capacity (2); XI-4 cost of capital →
investment → output (3 + the build lag); XI-17 the mandate (the term).

<!-- plan:gaps -->

## Part 4 — Owned implementation backlog

**955 clauses: 857 MISSING, 98 PARTIAL.** Generated from
`docs/COVERAGE.md` by `npm run plan:gaps`, ordered by the Part 1 milestone that owns each clause.
Every checkbox is one uniquely named to-do point and owns exactly one unmet requirement. Work
top-to-bottom by milestone; within a milestone, satisfy prerequisites before dependent points.
A MISSING clause is a mechanism nobody has written; a PARTIAL
one is a mechanism that exists and does not yet do all the clause says, and its row says what is
short. Neither is a finding — a finding is a defect in something that was built — and neither
waits on a measurement: an absence is not measurable (Audit E1), which is exactly why it has to be
in this executable list rather than in a table somebody reads later.

Re-mark the row in `docs/COVERAGE.md` in the change that meets it, and re-run `npm run plan:gaps`
in the same commit. Nothing here is ticked by hand. A point leaves this list only when its
coverage row becomes MET; therefore no MISSING or PARTIAL clause can be unowned.

### 10. Derivative — 10 missing, 5 partial

> **Required review before this block:** read the **Derivative** section of `docs/spec/PROJECT_PHOENIX.md` (requirements begin at line 964), then inspect `packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs`, `packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/derivative_layer.rs` and the registration in `packages/kernel-rs/src/systems.rs`. Re-read the relevant coverage row before each point; its note
> identifies known dead code, missing production callers, and verification evidence. Do not implement
> from this summary alone.

- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D1B** — `Derivative D1.b` MISSING — VERIFICATION 1.4: the ZeroSum family has no contribution and is declared NOT BUILT every period
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D3** — `Derivative D3` MISSING — `AgreementTerms::PriceForward` types its underlying as an instrument, but no production mechanism strikes that contract, so no live derivative can yet be checked against a price this world clears
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D3A** — `Derivative D3.a` MISSING — there is no underlying field, so the FORBID has nothing to refuse
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D4** — `Derivative D4` MISSING — no payoff function is carried on a contract; `derivative_layer.rs` variants are dead code
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D5** — `Derivative D5` MISSING — derivative terms now carry a typed settlement currency, and FX forwards carry both currencies, but a general derivative still has no typed payment-leg schedule with a currency on each leg
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D7** — `Derivative D7` MISSING — nothing strikes a derivative at a cleared level; `Derivatives` proposes nothing and opens no book (VERIFICATION 9.1)
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D8** — `Derivative D8` MISSING — VERIFICATION 6.3: nothing marks a contract after inception
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D9** — `Derivative D9` MISSING — `derivatives.collateral` is one of the three nouns with NO KERNEL HOME that `npm run world:runs` prints every run. `derivative_layer.rs initial_margin` and `variation` are dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1)
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D10** — `Derivative D10` MISSING — VERIFICATION 4.1: no claim is ever filed against an estate, so an in-the-money party has nothing
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.X2** — `Derivative X2` MISSING — VERIFICATION 9.1: `Derivatives` proposes no instruction, so no margin, premium or periodic payment leaves any account
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D1** — `Derivative D1` PARTIAL — packages/kernel-rs/src/mechanisms/derivative_layer.rs `agreed::DERIVATIVE` is a two-party agreement, so the two sides are named. Nothing makes it an asset to one and a liability to the other: no mark exists (VERIFICATION 6.3) and the Accounts family is NOT BUILT (1.4)
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D2** — `Derivative D2` PARTIAL — a notional can sit in an agreement`s terms. `derivative_layer.rs gross_notional` is dead code (VERIFICATION 11.1), so D2.a`s distinction between notional and exposure is drawn nowhere
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D6** — `Derivative D6` PARTIAL — packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements` carries an `until`, so a term can end. Payment dates on a leg are not represented, and `systems.rs` wires `derivative_layer::Derivatives`, but its production path does not perform this transition (item 10)
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D11** — `Derivative D11` PARTIAL — packages/kernel-rs/src/stores.rs `Agreements::ends` removes the relation from both parties at once. `derivative_layer.rs close_out` is dead code (item 10)
- [ ] **TODO 10.DERIVATIVE.D12** — `Derivative D12` PARTIAL — an agreement is its own row and each derivative class has typed identity fields, so an underlying/reference can no longer be confused with a strike or currency. The store does not yet enforce economic-key uniqueness (item 10)

What is missing is the other half: an investor that does not pay does not DEFAULT on it — `mechanisms/loss.rs` crosses a standing on a LINE and a bilateral obligation has none, so the row runs out of periods and no fund learns of it (item 10.7)

- [ ] **TODO 12.AUDIT.D4** — `Audit D4` MISSING — a Part XII measurement, and it needs families that are built (VERIFICATION 1.4)
- [ ] **TODO 12.AUDIT.B7** — `Audit B7` PARTIAL — packages/kernel-rs/src/audit.rs `FlowsAreComplete` compares every non-money holding`s change against the legs that said why it moved, over instructions that SETTLED. It does not cover money: a money leg does not state where its units land, and a family re-deriving settlement`s routing would be reading its answer (Audit C3) — that half is `MoneyIsConserved`, per currency. Per-account money flows are unchecked (item 12, when the routing is a read a family can take)
- [ ] **TODO 12.AUDIT.B8** — `Audit B8` PARTIAL — all ten families are built and separately reported; the distinct-family logic test prevents two report identities collapsing into one. The long-run one-defect experiment remains measurement work (item 14.1).
